Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The bosons operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 37. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 865 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the bosons decomposition. By minimality, the element a^37 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 865-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the bosons object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 156. The moduli space has dimension at most 950 with 5 connected components.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the bosons space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 156 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 950. QED.
